// ============================================================
// 🟥TRACKING_REQUEST🔴FINAL - BB EXPRESS SEND ROUTE - ปั้มแดงอย่างเดียว
// รองรับระบบ 7 เพจแบบ 100% ไดนามิก เช็กจากชื่อลูกค้าแทนการฟิกชื่อเพจ
// ============================================================

// ---------- 1. ตัวช่วยแปลงวันที่ไทยสับชิ้นส่วนเพียว ----------
function parseThaiDate(str) {
  if (!str) return null;
  const m = String(str).match(/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{2,4})\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
  if (!m) return null;
  let [_, d, mo, y, h, mi, s] = m;
  y = parseInt(y);
  if (y < 100) y += 2500; // 69 -> 2569
  if (y > 2400) y -= 543; // 2569 -> 2026
  return new Date(y, parseInt(mo) - 1, parseInt(d), parseInt(h), parseInt(mi), parseInt(s || 0));
}

function parseAnyDate(v) {
  if (!v) return null;
  if (v instanceof Date) return v;
  if (typeof v === 'number') return new Date(v);
  const d = new Date(v);
  if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) return d;
  return parseThaiDate(v);
}

// ---------- 2. ฟังก์ชั่นจับคำขอเลข (🛡️ ฉบับปิดจุดดักคำขอเลขเก่าข้ามวันข้ามคืน) ----------
function bindTrackingRequestToOrder(row, { maxDays = 3 } = {}) {
  const timeline = row.chat_timeline || row.chatTimeline || row.timeline || [];
  // 🎯 ดึงเวลาสากลหน้าจอหลักของพี่มาตั้งเป็นหมุดเวลาอ้างอิงหลัก
  const orderTime = parseAnyDate(row.order_time_display || row.order_time || row.order_time_thai_label || row["⏰ เวลาสั่งซื้อ"]);
  const currentCustomerName = String(row.facebook_name || row.customer_name || '').trim();

  const keywords = [
    "ขอเลข", "เลขพัสดุ", "เลขพัส", "เลขพัสดุผม", "เลขพัสดุหน่อย",
    "เช็คเลข", "เช็คของ", "ของส่งยัง", "ส้งแล้วขอเลข", "ดูไม่ได้",
    "เลขพัสดุได้ยัง", "ส่งยัง", "ได้เลขยัง", "ขอเลขหน่อย", "ขอเลขพัสดุ"
  ];

  let firstAskTime = null;
  let lastAskTime = null;
  let matchedText = null;

  const text = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline.join("\n") : String(timeline || "");
  const lines = text.split("\n");

  for (const line of lines) {
    if (!line.trim()) continue;

    const dateM = line.match(/(\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}\/\d{2,4}\s+\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?)/);
    const askDate = dateM ? parseThaiDate(dateM[0]) : null;
    const lower = line.toLowerCase();

    let isCustomer = false;
    const senderMatch = line.match(/^\[([^\]]+)\]/);
    if (senderMatch && currentCustomerName) {
      if (senderMatch[1].includes(currentCustomerName)) {
        isCustomer = true;
      }
    }

    const isAsk = keywords.some(k => lower.includes(k));

    // 🛡️ 🔥 [ลอจิกชี้ขาดความปลอดภัย]: คำขอเลขนั้นต้องเกิดขึ้น "หลัง" เวลาสั่งออเดอร์นี้เท่านั้น (askDate > orderTime)
    // ถ้าเวลาขอเลขมันเก่ากว่าเวลาบิลนี้ แปลว่าเป็นคำขอเลขของบิลเก่าในอดีต -> ดีดทิ้ง ไม่เอามาคำนวณให้ระบบเพี้ยน!
    if (isCustomer && isAsk && askDate && orderTime) {
      if (askDate >= orderTime) {
        if (!firstAskTime || askDate < firstAskTime) {
          firstAskTime = askDate;
          matchedText = line;
        }
        if (!lastAskTime || askDate > lastAskTime) lastAskTime = askDate;
      }
    }
  }

  if (!firstAskTime) return { detected: false, route: "PASS_THROUGH", diffHours: null, firstAskTime: null };

  let diffHours = null;
  let direction = "ASK_AFTER_ORDER"; // ล็อคสิทธิ์เป็นขอหลังสั่งชัวร์ 100%
  if (orderTime) {
    diffHours = Math.abs(firstAskTime - orderTime) / 3600000;
  }

  return {
    detected: true,
    route: "TRACKING_REQUEST",
    diffHours,
    direction,
    firstAskTime: firstAskTime.toISOString(),
    lastAskTime: lastAskTime ? lastAskTime.toISOString() : null,
    matchedText,
    orderTime: orderTime ? orderTime.toISOString() : null
  };
}

// ---------- 3. NODE2 MAIN ----------
return items.map(item => {
  const row = item.json;
  const originalRoute = row.bb_express_send_route;
  const originalBackup = row.status_color_backup || row.routing_tag_backup || "⚪White";

  // 🔎 💥 [ดักจับสัญญาณส่งเลขพัสดุออโต้หมุดแดงสั้นเพียว]
  const timelineContent = Array.isArray(row.chat_timeline) ? row.chat_timeline.join("\n") : String(row.chat_timeline || "");
  const latestMessageContent = String(row.page_latest_message || "");

  const hasAutoTrackingSignal = timelineContent.includes("📌") || latestMessageContent.includes("📌");

  const trackingCheck = bindTrackingRequestToOrder(row, { maxDays: 3 });
  row.tracking_check = trackingCheck;

  const askTime = trackingCheck.firstAskTime ? new Date(trackingCheck.firstAskTime) : null;
  const orderTime = trackingCheck.orderTime ? new Date(trackingCheck.orderTime) : parseAnyDate(row.order_time_display || row.order_time);

  row.status_color_backup = originalBackup;
  row.routing_tag_backup = originalBackup;
  row.is_safety_white = originalBackup === "⚪White";

  // ⚡ ด่านตัดสินผลลัพธ์แยกเส้นทาง
  if (hasAutoTrackingSignal) {
    // เพิ่งรับเลขไปหรือระบบพ่นหมุดหมุดแดงออโต้ให้ล่าสุด -> ปั้มแดงกลม 🔴 ทันที
    row.bb_express_send_route = "🔴";
    row.node2_route_signal = "TRACKING_REQUEST_AUTO";
    row.node2_tracking_request_detected = true;
    row.node2_tracking_request_48h = false;
    row.node2_tracking_request_event = "พบสัญญาณแจ้งเลขพัสดุอัตโนมัติ 📌";
    row.node2_tracking_request_route_hint = "🔴";
  } else if (trackingCheck.detected && askTime && orderTime) {
    const diffAfter = (askTime - orderTime) / 3600000;

    if (diffAfter < 48) {
      // ลูกค้าขอเลขภายใน 48 ชั่วโมงหลังสั่งซื้อ -> ต้องส่งเลข ปั้มแดงเหลี่ยม 🟥
      row.bb_express_send_route = "🟥";
      row.node2_route_signal = "TRACKING_REQUEST";
      row.node2_tracking_request_detected = true;
      row.node2_tracking_request_48h = true;
      row.node2_tracking_request_event = trackingCheck.matchedText;
      row.node2_tracking_request_route_hint = "🟥";
    } else {
      // นอกเหนือจากนั้น ดันเข้าแดงกลม 🔴
      row.bb_express_send_route = "🔴";
      row.node2_route_signal = "TRACKING_REQUEST";
      row.node2_tracking_request_detected = true;
      row.node2_tracking_request_48h = false;
      row.node2_tracking_request_event = trackingCheck.matchedText;
      row.node2_tracking_request_route_hint = "🔴";
    }
  } else {
    row.bb_express_send_route = originalRoute;
    row.node2_route_signal = "PASS_THROUGH";
    row.node2_tracking_request_detected = false;
    row.node2_tracking_request_48h = false;
  }

  // ปลั๊กอินคัดปลายทางแยกห้องเก็บตารางหลัก
  if (row.bb_express_send_route === "🟥" || row.bb_express_send_route === "🔴") {
    row.target_room = "RED_ROOM";
    row.routing_tag = row.bb_express_send_route === "🟥" ? "🟥 Red48" : "🔴 Red";
    row.status_color = row.bb_express_send_route === "🟥" ? "🟥 Red" : "🔴 Red";
  } else {
    row.target_room = "WHITE_ROOM";
  }

  // Tracking routing must not drop the canonical customer/page message fields.
  row.customer_message_text = row.customer_message_text ?? row.customer_latest_message ?? null;
  row.page_message_text = row.page_message_text ?? row.page_latest_message ?? null;

  return item;
});

// ============================================================
// MASTER ADDRESS CASCADE 3 SYSTEMS + ORDER FIBO + ADDRESS FIBO
// n8n Code Node — Run Once for All Items
// ============================================================
// Flow ภายในโหนด:
//   System 1 PRIMARY
//       ↓ ถ้าไม่ครบ
//   System 2 BACKUP
//       ↓ ถ้าไม่ครบ
//   System 3 CUSTOMER CHAT
//       ↓
//   Purifier → เลือก Address → Address Fibo
//       +
//   Order Fibo
//
// System 1: formatted_address / ค่าที่โหนดหน้าสกัดไว้แล้ว
// System 2: finalAddress / backup / address_candidates
// System 3: ข้อความลูกค้าจริงจาก chat_timeline
//
// ไม่ทำ:
// - ไม่ทำ Human/Bot Truth Gate
// - ไม่ส่ง Telegram
// - ไม่ลบ original/chat_timeline/raw
// - ไม่เอาคะแนน Order Fibo มาปน Address Fibo
// ============================================================

const items = $input.all();

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Utilities
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function asText(v) {
  if (v === undefined || v === null) return '';
  if (typeof v === 'object') {
    try { return JSON.stringify(v); } catch (_) { return String(v); }
  }
  return String(v)
    .normalize('NFKC')
    .replace(/\\r\\n/g, '\n')
    .replace(/\\n/g, '\n')
    .replace(/\r\n/g, '\n')
    .trim();
}
function oneLine(v) { return asText(v).replace(/\s+/gu, ' ').trim(); }
function digits(v) { return oneLine(v).replace(/\D/g, ''); }
function numberValue(v) {
  const n = Number(String(v ?? '').replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, ''));
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}
function parseLines(v) {
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(asText).filter(Boolean);
  const s = asText(v);
  if (!s) return [];
  try {
    const x = JSON.parse(s);
    return Array.isArray(x) ? x.map(asText).filter(Boolean) : [s];
  } catch (_) {
    return s.split(/\n+/u).map(asText).filter(Boolean);
  }
}
function isMissing(v) {
  return /^(?:DATA_MISSING|DATA\s*MISSING|N\/A|NA|null|undefined|ไม่มีข้อมูล|-|_)$/iu.test(oneLine(v));
}

function parsePackerObject(value) {
  if (value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)) return value;
  const raw = asText(value);
  if (!raw) return {};
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : {};
  } catch (_) {
    return {};
  }
}

function standaloneHouseToken(value) {
  const s = oneLine(value);
  return /^(?:บ้านเลขที่\s*)?\d{1,5}(?:[/-]\d{1,5})?(?:\s+(?:หมู่(?:ที่)?\s*\d+|ม\.\s*\d+))?$/iu.test(s) ? s : '';
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Purifier / Guard
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function removeNameAndPhoneLines(value, phoneValue) {
  const phone = digits(phoneValue);
  return asText(value)
    .split(/\n+/u)
    .filter(line => {
      const normalized = oneLine(line);
      if (!normalized) return false;
      if (phone.length >= 9 && digits(normalized) === phone) return false;
      if (/^\+?\d[\d\s().-]{8,}$/u.test(normalized)) return false;
      return true;
    })
    .join(' ')
    .trim();
}
function isNameOnlyAddress(value) {
  const s = asText(value);
  return /^(?:ชื่อผู้รับ|ชื่อ|ผู้รับ)\s*[:：]/iu.test(s)
    && !/(?:บ้านเลขที่|เลขที่|หมู่|หมู่บ้าน|ม\.|ต\.|ตำบล|อ\.|อำเภอ|จ\.|จังหวัด|แขวง|เขต|ถนน|ซอย|\d{5})/iu.test(s);
}
function stripTimelinePrefix(value) {
  return asText(value)
    .replace(/^\[[^\]]*\]\s*/u, '')
    .replace(/^\d{1,2}[/-]\d{1,2}[/-]\d{2,4}\s+\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?\]\s*/u, '')
    .replace(/^\d{1,2}[/-]\d{1,2}[/-]\d{2,4}\s+\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?\s*/u, '')
    .trim();
}

function stripToAddressPayload(rawValue, customerName, phoneValue) {
  let s = removeNameAndPhoneLines(stripTimelinePrefix(rawValue), phoneValue);
  if (!s || isMissing(s) || isNameOnlyAddress(s)) return '';

  // ที่อยู่จัดส่ง/ที่อยู่ เป็น anchor สูงสุด
  const labeled = s.match(/(?:ที่อยู่จัดส่ง|ที่อยู่|address)\s*[:：]\s*([\s\S]*)/iu);
  if (labeled) s = labeled[1];

  // ลบชื่อผู้รับที่วางไว้หน้าที่อยู่ ถ้ารู้ชื่อ
  const customer = asText(customerName);
  if (customer && s.startsWith(customer)) s = s.slice(customer.length).trim();

  // ตัดสินค้า/ขนส่ง/แฮชแท็ก
  s = s.split(/(?:📦\s*รายการสินค้า|รายการสินค้า\s*[:：]|🚚\s*ขนส่ง|#ORDER|ORDER_MYSTORY|◦◦◦|━━━━━━━━)/iu)[0];

  // หยุดก่อนโทร/เบอร์/มือถือ
  s = s.split(/(?:=|\s)(?:โทรศัพท์|โทร|เบอร์|มือถือ)\s*[:：=]?/iu)[0];

  // หา Address anchor จริง ไม่ใช้วันที่/COD/ชื่อสินค้าเป็นจุดเริ่ม
  const starts = [...s.matchAll(/(?:บ้านเลขที่\s*\d+[\/]?\d*|เลขที่\s*\d+[\/]?\d*|\b\d{1,5}\/\d{1,5}\b|หมู่บ้าน|หมู่(?:ที่)?\s*\d+|\bม\.\s*\d+|วัด|สำนักสงฆ์|ที่พักสงฆ์|ศาลเจ้า|หอพัก|คอนโด|อาคาร|บ้านพัก|ตําบล|ตำบล|ต\.|อําเภอ|อำเภอ|อ\.|จังหวัด|จ\.|แขวง|เขต|ถนน|ถ\.|ซอย|ซ\.)/giu)]
    .map(m => ({ index: m.index, token: m[0] }))
    .filter(x => Number.isInteger(x.index));

  if (starts.length && !/^\d{1,5}(?:\/|-)?\s*\d{0,5}\b/u.test(s.trim())) {
    const strong = starts.find(x => /(?:บ้านเลขที่|เลขที่|\d{1,5}\/\d{1,5}|หมู่บ้าน|หมู่|\bม\.)/iu.test(x.token));
    s = s.slice((strong || starts[0]).index);
  }

  // ลบเบอร์เต็มที่หลุดใน payload
// ลบเฉพาะเลขท้ายเบอร์ที่ติดหน้าชื่อสถานที่
// ต้องตรงกับท้ายเบอร์ผู้รับ และตามด้วยคำบอกสถานที่/เขตปกครอง
const phoneDigits = digits(phoneValue);

if (phoneDigits.length >= 9) {
  const landmarkStart =
    String.raw`(?:ที่พักสงฆ์|สำนักสงฆ์|วัด|สำนัก|ศูนย์|ตำบล|ต\.|อำเภอ|อ\.|จังหวัด|จ\.|แขวง|เขต|หมู่บ้าน|โรงเรียน|ตลาด|โรงพยาบาล|บริษัท|หอพัก|คอนโด|ถนน|ซอย)`;

  const leadingPhoneTail = s.match(
    new RegExp(`^\\s*(\\d{4,9})\\s+(?=${landmarkStart})`, 'iu')
  );

  if (leadingPhoneTail && phoneDigits.endsWith(leadingPhoneTail[1])) {
    s = s.slice(leadingPhoneTail[0].length).trim();
  }
}

  return s
    .replace(/[=|]+/gu, ' ')
    .replace(/ตําบล/gu, 'ตำบล')
    .replace(/อําเภอ/gu, 'อำเภอ')
    .replace(/(?:กทม\.?\s*,?\s*)?(?:จ\.?\s*)?กรุงเทพมหานคร/giu, 'กรุงเทพมหานคร')
    .replace(/(?:กรุงเทพ(?!มหานคร)(?:ฯ)?|กทม\.?(?:ฯ)?)/giu, 'กรุงเทพมหานคร')
    .replace(/\s+/gu, ' ')
    .trim();
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// System 3: customer chat only
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function parseTimelineEntry(value) {
  const line = asText(value);
  const m = line.match(/^\[([^:]+):\s*[^\]]+\]\s*([\s\S]*)$/u);
  if (!m) return { sender: '', body: stripTimelinePrefix(line) };
  return { sender: asText(m[1]), body: stripTimelinePrefix(m[2]) };
}
function isBotTimeline(sender, body) {
  return /(?:ร้าน|page|store|admin|แอดมิน|bot|ทีมงาน|หนูส่ง|สรุปรายการ|ปิดยอดสำเร็จ|รายการสินค้า|COD\s*[:：])/iu.test(`${sender} ${body}`);
}
function system3Evidence(row) {
  return parseLines(row.chat_timeline)
    .map(parseTimelineEntry)
    .filter(x => x.body)
    .filter(x => !isBotTimeline(x.sender, x.body))
    .filter(x => /(?:หมู่|บ้าน|เลขที่|ตําบล|ตำบล|ต\.|อําเภอ|อำเภอ|อ\.|จังหวัด|จ\.|แขวง|เขต|ถนน|ซอย|\d{5}|โทร|เบอร์)/iu.test(x.body))
    .map(x => ({
      system: 'SYSTEM_3_CUSTOMER_CHAT',
      field: 'chat_timeline.customer_message',
      sender: x.sender,
      raw: x.body
    }));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// System 1 / System 2 candidates
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function system1Candidates(row) {
  const packer = parsePackerObject(row.packer_obj ?? row.packerObj);
  return [
    ['formatted_address', row.formatted_address],
    ['address_display_packer', row.address_display_packer],
    ['addressclean', row.addressclean],
    ['full_address', row.full_address],
    ['final_address_for_bill', row.final_address_for_bill],
    ['packer_obj.formatted_address', packer.formatted_address],
    ['packer_obj.address_display_packer', packer.address_display_packer],
    ['packer_obj.final_address_for_bill', packer.final_address_for_bill],
    ['packer_obj.finalAddress', packer.finalAddress],
    ['packer_obj.full_address', packer.full_address],
    ['packer_obj.addressclean', packer.addressclean],
    ['packer_obj.address', packer.address],
    ['packer_obj.short_address', packer.short_address]
  ]
    .filter(([, value]) => value !== undefined && value !== null && !isMissing(value))
    .map(([field, raw]) => ({ system: 'SYSTEM_1_UPSTREAM', field, sender: '', raw: asText(raw) }))
    .filter(candidate => !candidate.field.startsWith('packer_obj.') || !standaloneHouseToken(candidate.raw));
}
function system2Candidates(row) {
  const out = [];
  const values = [
    ['finalAddress', row.finalAddress],
    ['full_address_backup_1', row.full_address_backup_1],
    ['full_address_backup_2', row.full_address_backup_2]
  ];
  for (const [field, value] of values) {
    if (value !== undefined && value !== null && !isMissing(value)) {
      out.push({ system: 'SYSTEM_2_FUSION_BACKUP', field, sender: '', raw: asText(value) });
    }
  }
  for (const value of parseLines(row.address_candidates)) {
    const raw = typeof value === 'object' ? (value.clean || value.raw || '') : value;
    if (raw && !isMissing(raw)) out.push({ system: 'SYSTEM_2_FUSION_BACKUP', field: 'address_candidates', sender: '', raw: asText(raw) });
  }
  for (const value of parseLines(row.address_candidates_all)) {
    const raw = typeof value === 'object' ? (value.clean || value.raw || '') : value;
    if (raw && !isMissing(raw)) out.push({ system: 'SYSTEM_2_FUSION_BACKUP', field: 'address_candidates_all', sender: '', raw: asText(raw) });
  }
  return out;
}

function system4RescueCandidates(row) {
  const rescueZip = [row.rescue_zipcode, row.clean_zipcode, row.zipcode]
    .map(value => asText(value).match(/(?<!\d)([1-9]\d{4})(?!\d)/u)?.[1] || '')
    .find(Boolean) || '';

  const values = [
    ['rescue_address_normalized', row.rescue_address_normalized],
    ['telegram_primary_address', row.telegram_primary_address],
    ['rescue_address', row.rescue_address],
    ['clean_address', row.clean_address],
    ['rescue_address_raw', row.rescue_address_raw]
  ];

  return values
    .filter(([, value]) => value !== undefined && value !== null && !isMissing(value))
    .map(([field, value]) => {
      const raw = asText(value);
      const hasZip = /(?<!\d)[1-9]\d{4}(?!\d)/u.test(raw);
      return {
        system: 'SYSTEM_4_RESCUE_NODE',
        field,
        sender: 'RESCUE_NODE',
        raw: rescueZip && !hasZip ? `${raw} ${rescueZip}` : raw
      };
    });
}

function assessCandidate(candidate, row) {
  const clean = stripToAddressPayload(
    candidate.raw,
    row.customer_name || row.facebook_name,
    row.phone || row.extracted_phone
  );
  if (!clean || isMissing(clean) || isNameOnlyAddress(clean)) {
    return { ...candidate, raw: candidate.raw, clean: '', score: -999, complete: false, usable: false, issues: ['NON_ADDRESS_CANDIDATE'], address_type: 'UNKNOWN' };
  }
function removeThaiDistrictProvincePrefixes(addressText) {
  let s = asText(addressText);
  if (!s) return '';

  // ตัดคำว่า ตำบล, อำเภอ, จังหวัด และตัวย่อออกให้เกลี้ยง เหลือแต่ชื่อสถานที่และตัวเลข
  s = s
    .replace(/(?:ตำบล|ตํำบล|ตําบล|ต\.|ต\s)/gu, '')
    .replace(/(?:อำเภอ|อําเภอ|อ\.|อ\s)/gu, '')
    .replace(/(?:จังหวัด|จ\.|จ\s)/gu, '')
    .replace(/(?:แขวง)/gu, '')
    .replace(/(?:เขต)/gu, '')
    .replace(/\s+/gu, ' ') // ยุบช่องว่างที่เกินให้เหลือช่องว่างเดียว
    .trim();

  return s;
}
  const isBangkok = /(?:กรุงเทพมหานคร|กรุงเทพ(?!มหานคร)(?:ฯ)?|กทม\.?)/iu.test(clean);
  const hasHouse = /(?:บ้านเลขที่|เลขที่|^\d{1,5}(?:\/|-)?\s*\d{0,5}\b|\d+\s*ม\.?|อาคาร|ห้อง)/iu.test(clean);
  const hasVillage = /(?:หมู่บ้าน|หมู่(?:ที่)?\s*\d+|\bม\.\s*\d+)/iu.test(clean);
  const hasLocal = isBangkok ? /แขวง\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean) : /(?:ตำบล|ต\.)\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean);
  const hasDistrict = isBangkok ? /เขต\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean) : /(?:อำเภอ|อ\.)\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean);
  const hasProvince = isBangkok || /(?:จังหวัด|จ\.)\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean);
  const hasZip = /(?<!\d)[1-9]\d{4}(?!\d)/u.test(clean);
  const contaminated = /(?:COD|ยอด\s*COD|รายการสินค้า|คอต\.?|ขนส่ง|ORDER_MYSTORY|หนูส่ง|ทีมงาน)/iu.test(clean);
  const landmark = /(?:วัด|สำนักสงฆ์|ร้านอาหาร|ร้านกาแฟ|ร้านตัดผม|ร้านค้า|ตลาด|โรงเรียน|บริษัท|หอพัก|รีสอร์ท|โรงแรม|คลินิก|โรงพยาบาล|ปั๊ม|ฟาร์ม|สวน)/iu.test(clean);
  const landmarkUsable = landmark && (hasLocal || hasDistrict || hasProvince || hasZip);

  const complete = hasHouse && hasZip && hasLocal && hasDistrict && hasProvince;
  const usable = complete || landmarkUsable || (hasLocal && (hasDistrict || hasProvince));
  const issues = [];
  if (!hasHouse) issues.push('MISSING_HOUSE_NUMBER');
  if (!hasZip) issues.push('MISSING_ZIPCODE');
  if (!hasLocal) issues.push(isBangkok ? 'MISSING_KHWANG' : 'MISSING_TAMBON');
  if (!hasDistrict) issues.push(isBangkok ? 'MISSING_KHET' : 'MISSING_AMPHOE');
  if (!hasProvince) issues.push('MISSING_PROVINCE');
  if (contaminated) issues.push('ADDRESS_CONTAMINATION');
  if (landmarkUsable && !complete) issues.push('LANDMARK_ADDRESS_REVIEW');

  return {
    ...candidate,
    clean,
    complete,
    usable,
    landmark_address: landmarkUsable,
    has_house_number: hasHouse,
    has_zipcode: hasZip,
    has_local_area: hasLocal,
    has_district_area: hasDistrict,
    has_province: hasProvince,
    contaminated,
    issues,
    address_type: isBangkok ? 'BANGKOK_KHWANG_KHET' : 'PROVINCE_TAMBON_AMPHOE',
    score: 0
  };
}

function addressCandidateQuality(candidate) {
  return (candidate?.has_house_number ? 5 : 0)
    + (candidate?.landmark_address ? 4 : 0)
    + (candidate?.has_zipcode ? 1 : 0)
    + (candidate?.has_local_area ? 1 : 0)
    + (candidate?.has_district_area ? 1 : 0)
    + (candidate?.has_province ? 1 : 0)
    - (candidate?.contaminated ? 5 : 0);
}

function cascadeAddress(row) {
  const s1 = system1Candidates(row).map(c => assessCandidate(c, row)).filter(c => c.clean);
  const s2 = system2Candidates(row).map(c => assessCandidate(c, row)).filter(c => c.clean);
  const s3 = system3Evidence(row).map(c => assessCandidate(c, row)).filter(c => c.clean);
  const s4 = system4RescueCandidates(row).map(c => assessCandidate(c, row)).filter(c => c.clean);

  const firstComplete = list => list.find(x => x.complete === true);
  const firstUsable = list => list.find(x => x.usable === true);

  // คงลำดับเดิมของ Systems 1–3; Rescue เป็น System 4
  let selected = firstComplete(s1) || firstComplete(s2) || firstComplete(s3) || firstComplete(s4)
    || firstUsable(s1) || firstUsable(s2) || firstUsable(s3) || firstUsable(s4)
    || s1[0] || s2[0] || s3[0] || s4[0]
    || { system: 'NONE', field: '', sender: '', raw: '', clean: '', score: -999, complete: false, usable: false, issues: ['ADDRESS_NOT_FOUND'], address_type: 'UNKNOWN' };

  // If the preferred candidate is incomplete, upgrade only to a richer usable packer_obj landmark address.
  if (!selected.complete) {
    const richerPackerAddress = s1
      .filter(candidate => candidate.field.startsWith('packer_obj.') && candidate.usable && !candidate.contaminated && candidate.landmark_address)
      .sort((a, b) => addressCandidateQuality(b) - addressCandidateQuality(a))[0];
    if (richerPackerAddress && addressCandidateQuality(richerPackerAddress) > addressCandidateQuality(selected)) {
      selected = richerPackerAddress;
    }
  }

  // A standalone house number from packer_obj is a fragment, not a full candidate.
  // Merge it with the selected location only when that location has no house number.
  const packer = parsePackerObject(row.packer_obj ?? row.packerObj);
  const packerHouse = [packer.house_number, packer.house_no, packer.house, packer.address, packer.short_address]
    .map(standaloneHouseToken)
    .find(Boolean) || '';
  if (packerHouse && selected.clean && !selected.has_house_number) {
    const merged = assessCandidate({
      ...selected,
      field: `packer_obj.house_number+${selected.field || 'selected_address'}`,
      raw: `${packerHouse} ${selected.clean}`
    }, row);
    if (merged.clean) selected = merged;
  }

  const level = selected.system === 'SYSTEM_1_UPSTREAM' ? 'PRIMARY'
    : selected.system === 'SYSTEM_2_FUSION_BACKUP' ? 'FALLBACK_1'
    : selected.system === 'SYSTEM_3_CUSTOMER_CHAT' ? 'FALLBACK_2'
    : selected.system === 'SYSTEM_4_RESCUE_NODE' ? 'FALLBACK_3'
    : 'RAW_FALLBACK';

  return {
    selected,
    system1: s1,
    system2: s2,
    system3: s3,
    system4: s4,
    all: [...s1, ...s2, ...s3, ...s4],
    fallback_used: level !== 'PRIMARY',
    fallback_level: level,
    selection_reason: selected.complete
      ? `${selected.system}_COMPLETE`
      : selected.usable
        ? `${selected.system}_USABLE_REVIEW`
        : `${selected.system}_PARTIAL_REVIEW`
  };
}

function addressFibo(address) {
  const clean = asText(address?.clean);
  if (!clean) {
    return { score: 0, tier: '🔴 ADDRESS_EMPTY', badge: '📍 ADDRESS-FIBO-0 [🔴 ADDRESS_EMPTY]', reason: 'DATA_MISSING', usable: false, complete: false };
  }

  const isBangkok = /กรุงเทพมหานคร/iu.test(clean);
  const hasHouse = /(?:บ้านเลขที่|เลขที่|^\d{1,5}(?:\/|-)?\s*\d{0,5}\b|\d+\s*ม\.?|อาคาร|ห้อง)/iu.test(clean);
  const hasVillage = /(?:หมู่บ้าน|หมู่(?:ที่)?\s*\d+|\bม\.\s*\d+)/iu.test(clean);
  const hasLocal = isBangkok ? /แขวง\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean) : /(?:ตำบล|ต\.)\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean);
  const hasDistrict = isBangkok ? /เขต\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean) : /(?:อำเภอ|อ\.)\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean);
  const hasProvince = isBangkok || /(?:จังหวัด|จ\.)\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(clean);
  const hasZip = /(?<!\d)[1-9]\d{4}(?!\d)/u.test(clean);
  const contamination = /(?:COD|รายการสินค้า|คอต\.?|ขนส่ง|ORDER_MYSTORY|หนูส่ง|ทีมงาน)/iu.test(clean);
  const landmark = /(?:วัด|สำนักสงฆ์|ร้านอาหาร|ร้านกาแฟ|ร้านตัดผม|ร้านค้า|ตลาด|โรงเรียน|บริษัท|หอพัก|รีสอร์ท|โรงแรม|คลินิก|โรงพยาบาล|ปั๊ม|ฟาร์ม|สวน)/iu.test(clean);
  const landmarkUsable = landmark && (hasLocal || hasDistrict || hasProvince || hasZip);

  let score = 2;
  const reasons = ['ADDRESS_TEXT'];
  if (hasHouse) { score += 5; reasons.push('HOUSE_+5'); }
  if (hasVillage) { score += 3; reasons.push('VILLAGE_+3'); }
  if (hasLocal) { score += 8; reasons.push(isBangkok ? 'KHWANG_+8' : 'TAMBON_+8'); }
  if (hasDistrict) { score += 13; reasons.push(isBangkok ? 'KHET_+13' : 'AMPHOE_+13'); }
  if (hasProvince) { score += 21; reasons.push('PROVINCE_+21'); }
  if (hasZip) { score += 34; reasons.push('ZIPCODE_+34'); }
  if (!contamination) { score += 5; reasons.push('CLEAN_+5'); }
  if (landmarkUsable) { score += 8; reasons.push('LANDMARK_+8'); }

  const complete = hasHouse && hasZip && hasLocal && hasDistrict && hasProvince;
  const usable = complete || landmarkUsable || (hasLocal && (hasDistrict || hasProvince));
  const issues = address?.issues ? [...address.issues] : [];
  if (!hasHouse && !issues.includes('MISSING_HOUSE_NUMBER')) issues.push('MISSING_HOUSE_NUMBER');
  if (!hasZip && !issues.includes('MISSING_ZIPCODE')) issues.push('MISSING_ZIPCODE');
  if (!hasLocal && !issues.includes(isBangkok ? 'MISSING_KHWANG' : 'MISSING_TAMBON')) issues.push(isBangkok ? 'MISSING_KHWANG' : 'MISSING_TAMBON');
  if (!hasDistrict && !issues.includes(isBangkok ? 'MISSING_KHET' : 'MISSING_AMPHOE')) issues.push(isBangkok ? 'MISSING_KHET' : 'MISSING_AMPHOE');
  if (!hasProvince && !issues.includes('MISSING_PROVINCE')) issues.push('MISSING_PROVINCE');
  if (landmarkUsable && !complete && !issues.includes('LANDMARK_ADDRESS_REVIEW')) issues.push('LANDMARK_ADDRESS_REVIEW');

  let tier = '🔴 ADDRESS_REVIEW';
  if (score >= 89) tier = '👑 ADDRESS_ULTIMATE';
  else if (score >= 55) tier = '🌟 ADDRESS_GOLD';
  else if (score >= 34) tier = '🏆 ADDRESS_USABLE';
  else if (score >= 21) tier = '💵 ADDRESS_PARTIAL';

  return {
    score,
    tier,
    badge: `📍 ADDRESS-FIBO-${score} [${tier}]`,
    reason: reasons.join('|'),
    usable,
    complete,
    landmark: landmarkUsable,
    issues,
    type: isBangkok ? 'BANGKOK_KHWANG_KHET' : 'PROVINCE_TAMBON_AMPHOE'
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Order Fibo — แยก namespace จาก Address Fibo
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function productText(row) {
  return oneLine(row.trusted_product || row.for_packer_st_display || row.telegram_product_display || row.orderItems || row.sku || '');
}
function orderQty(row, product) {
  const explicit = numberValue(row.final_order_qty || row.extracted_text_qty || row.qty_display_th);
  if (explicit > 0) return explicit;
  const m = product.match(/(?:x|×|จำนวน)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/iu)
    || product.match(/(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(?:คอต|คอต\.|ซอง|ชิ้น|กล่อง|แพ็ค|แพ็ก)/iu);
  return m ? Number(m[1]) : 0;
}
function orderFibo(row) {
  const product = productText(row);
  const qty = orderQty(row, product);
  const nodePrice = numberValue(row.final_order_price || row.node_price || row.cod_amount);
  const extractedPrice = numberValue(row.extracted_text_price || row.expected_cod);
  const effectivePrice = nodePrice > 0 ? nodePrice : extractedPrice;
  const source = [row.SNIPER_X_TEXT, row.original, row.original_api_message].filter(Boolean).map(asText).join('\n');

  let score = 0;
  if (effectivePrice >= 2000) score += 55;
  else if (effectivePrice >= 1000) score += 34;
  else if (effectivePrice >= 800) score += 21;
  else if (effectivePrice >= 400) score += 13;
  else if (effectivePrice > 200) score += 8;

  if (qty >= 10) score += 34;
  else if (qty >= 5) score += 21;
  else if (qty >= 2) score += 8;
  else score += 2;

  if (row.order_number || row.id) score += 3;
  if (row.customer_name || row.name || row.phone || row.extracted_phone) score += 3;
  if (source.toLowerCase().includes('#singto_order')) score += 5;
  if (source.toLowerCase().includes('cod') || row.is_cod === true) score += 3;
  if (source.includes('สรุปยอด') || source.includes('ปิดยอดสำเร็จ')) score += 5;

  let saltyGold = false;
  let silentWhale = false;
  if (effectivePrice >= 1500 && source.trim().length < 15) { score += 21; silentWhale = true; }
  if (qty === 1 && effectivePrice >= 1000) { score += 13; saltyGold = true; }

  let tier = '💲 21_GOLDEN_FLOOR';
  let mathematicalPrice = 200;
  if (score >= 89) { tier = '👑 144_ULTIMATE_VIP'; mathematicalPrice = 2000; }
  else if (score >= 55) { tier = '🌟 89_GOLDEN_TOP'; mathematicalPrice = 1000; }
  else if (score >= 34) { tier = '🏆 55_GOLDEN_RATIO'; mathematicalPrice = 700; }
  else if (score >= 21) { tier = '💵 34_GOLDEN_MEDIUM'; mathematicalPrice = 400; }

  let vipTag = '🔰 BRONZE_FOOT_SOLDIER';
  let vip = false;
  if (score >= 89) { vipTag = '👑 ULTIMATE_VIP_EXPRESS'; vip = true; }
  else if (score >= 55) { vipTag = '🌟 GOLDEN_TOP_EXPRESS'; vip = true; }
  else if (score >= 34) { vipTag = '🏆 GOLDEN_RATIO_COMMANDER'; vip = true; }
  else if (score >= 21) vipTag = '💵 MEDIUM_SOLDIER';
  if (saltyGold) vipTag = `🧈 GOLDEN_TURBO [${vipTag}]`;

  return {
    score,
    tier,
    badge: `🔥 FIBO-${score} [${tier}]`,
    mathematicalPrice,
    finalOrderPrice: nodePrice > 0 ? nodePrice : (extractedPrice > 0 ? extractedPrice : mathematicalPrice),
    effectivePrice,
    qty,
    product,
    saltyGold,
    silentWhale,
    vip,
    vipTag
  };
}
function removeThaiDistrictProvincePrefixes(addressText) {
  let s = asText(addressText);
  if (!s) return '';

  // ตัดคำว่า ตำบล, อำเภอ, จังหวัด และตัวย่อออกให้เกลี้ยง เหลือแต่ชื่อสถานที่และตัวเลข
  s = s
.replace(/(?:^|\s)(?:ตำบล|ตํำบล|ตําบล|ต\.)\s*/gu, ' ') // ตัดเฉพาะคำว่า ตำบล หรือ ต. ที่นำหน้าด้วยเว้นวรรคหรือจุดเริ่มต้น
    .replace(/(?:^|\s)(?:อำเภอ|อําเภอ|อ\.)\s*/gu, ' ')     // ตัดเฉพาะ อำเภอ หรือ อ.
    .replace(/(?:^|\s)(?:จังหวัด|จ\.)\s*/gu, ' ')          // ตัดเฉพาะ จังหวัด หรือ จ.
    .replace(/\s+/gu, ' ')                                // ยุบช่องว่างส่วนเกิน
    .trim();

  return s;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Main
// ------------------------------------------------------------
const output = [];

for (const item of items) {
  const row = item?.json || {};
  const cascade = cascadeAddress(row);
  const selected = cascade.selected;
  const address = addressFibo(selected);
  const order = orderFibo(row);

  output.push({
    json: {
      ...row,
      // Preserve the canonical Chat contract through the address/order lane.
      // Do not use page/admin text as a customer trigger.
      customer_message_text: asText(row.customer_message_text || row.customer_latest_message) || null,
      page_message_text: asText(row.page_message_text || row.page_latest_message) || null,
// ---------------- CASCADE RESULT ----------------

final_address_for_bill: removeThaiDistrictProvincePrefixes(selected.clean) || removeThaiDistrictProvincePrefixes(row.final_address_for_bill) || '',

      address_raw_source_line: selected.raw || '',
      address_selected_system: selected.system,
      address_selected_field: selected.field,
      address_customer_sender: selected.sender || '',
      address_fallback_used: cascade.fallback_used,
      address_fallback_level: cascade.fallback_level,
      address_selection_reason: cascade.selection_reason,
      address_validation_status: selected.complete ? 'ADDRESS_OK' : 'ADDRESS_REVIEW',
      address_validation_complete: selected.complete === true,
      address_usable: selected.usable === true,
      address_capture_status: selected.landmark_address ? 'LANDMARK_CAPTURED_REVIEW' : (selected.complete ? 'COMPLETE' : 'PARTIAL_REVIEW'),
      address_validation_issues: selected.issues || [],
      address_type: selected.address_type || 'UNKNOWN',
      address_system_1_result: cascade.system1,
      address_system_2_result: cascade.system2,
      address_system_3_result: cascade.system3,
      address_system_4_result: cascade.system4,
      address_candidates_all: cascade.all,
      address_raw_retention: 'KEEP',

      // ---------------- ADDRESS FIBO ----------------
      address_fibo_score: address.score,

      address_fibo_cleaner: 'CASCADE_PURIFIER_V1',

      // ---------------- ORDER FIBO ----------------
      fibo_score: order.score,
      fibo_tier: order.tier,
      fibo_badge: order.badge,

    }

// ============================================================
// V6 - MASTER PRODUCT MATCHING + RAW PRESERVATION + DYNAMIC QTY
// ============================================================
const PRODUCT_MASTER = Object.fromEntries(PRODUCT_MASTER_ARR.map(p => [p.sku, p]));

function value(r, k) {
  const v = r?.[k];
  return v === undefined || v === null ? '' : String(v).trim();
}
function firstExisting(r, keys) {
  if (!Array.isArray(keys)) return '';
  for (const k of keys) {
    const v = value(r, k);
    if (v) return v;
  }
  return '';
}
function parseNumber(raw) {
  if (raw == null || String(raw).trim() === '') return null;
  const n = Number(String(raw).replaceAll(',', '').replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, ''));
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
}
function formatBaht(n) {
  const num = parseNumber(n);
  return num == null ? '' : new Intl.NumberFormat('th-TH').format(num);
}
function escapeHtml(v) {
  return String(v ?? '').replaceAll('&','&amp;').replaceAll('<','&lt;').replaceAll('>','&gt;').replaceAll('"','&quot;');
}

// ตัดแต่งข้อความเพื่อใช้เทียบ SKU (ตัดจำนวนและคำรบออกให้เหลือแต่ชื่อหลัก)
function productPhrase(raw) {
  return String(raw ?? '')
    .normalize('NFKC')
    .replace(/จํานวน/gu, 'จำนวน')
    .replace(/\|.*$/u, '')
    .replace(/\b\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?\s*(?:THB|บาท)\b.*$/iu, '')
    .replace(/\s*[-–—]\s*(?:จำนวน\s*)?\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*(?:คอต|คอท|ชิ้น|กล่อง|แพ็ค|แพ็ก|ซอง)?/giu, ' ')
    .replace(/(?:จำนวน|มี|x|×)\s*\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*(?:คอต|คอท|ชิ้น|กล่อง|แพ็ค|แพ็ก|ซอง)?/giu, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+[1-9][0-9]*\s*$/u, '')
    .replace(/(?:ยอด\s*(?:รวม\s*)?COD|COD\s*:?|รหัสไปรษณีย์).*$/iu, '')
    .replace(/\s+/gu, ' ')
    .trim();
}

function productKey(raw) {
  return productPhrase(raw)
    .replace(/[|｜:：=]+/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*(?:คอต|คอท|ชิ้น|กล่อง|แพ็ค|แพค|กระป๋อง|ซอง)\.?/giu, ' ')
    .replace(/\bx\s*\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b/giu, ' ')
    .replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9ก-๙_\s&.]+/gu, ' ')
    .replace(/\s*[_\s.&]+/gu, '')
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase();
}

const compactKeyCache = new Map();
function compactKey(raw) {
  const source = String(raw ?? '');
  if (compactKeyCache.has(source)) return compactKeyCache.get(source);
  const result = productKey(source).replace(/[ _.&]/g, '');
  compactKeyCache.set(source, result);
  return result;
}

const masterIndex = new Map();
for (const master of PRODUCT_MASTER_ARR) {
  const keys = [master.sku, master.name, master.thName, ...(master.aliases || [])].map(compactKey).filter(Boolean);
  for (const key of keys) {
    const previous = masterIndex.get(key);
    if (!previous) masterIndex.set(key, master);
    else if (previous.sku !== master.sku) masterIndex.set(key, null);
  }
}

const system2Cache = new Map();
function system2Master(raw) {
  const source = String(raw ?? '');
  if (system2Cache.has(source)) return system2Cache.get(source);
  const key = compactKey(source);
  if (!key) { system2Cache.set(source, null); return null; }
  const candidates = PRODUCT_MASTER_ARR.filter(master => {
    const brands = (master.brand || []).map(compactKey).filter(Boolean);
    const variants = (master.variant || []).map(compactKey).filter(Boolean);
    return brands.some(brand => key.includes(brand)) && variants.some(variant => key.includes(variant));
  });
  const result = candidates.length === 1 ? candidates[0] : null;
  system2Cache.set(source, result);
  return result;
}

function isProductLineCandidate(line) {
  const s = String(line ?? '').normalize('NFKC').replace(/จํานวน/gu, 'จำนวน').trim();
  if (!s) return false;
  if (/(?:สรุปรายการสั่งซื้อ|เลขที่ออเดอร์|เวลาสั่งซื้อ|ชื่อผู้รับ|เบอร์โทร|ที่อยู่จัดส่ง|รหัสไปรษณีย์|ยอดรวม\s*COD|COD\s*:?|ขนส่ง|โหนดส่ง|ทีมจะ)/iu.test(s)) return false;
  const trailingQty = s.replace(/(?:\s|^)(?:จำนวน|มี|x|×)?\s*[1-9][0-9]*(?:\.0)?\s*$/iu, '').trim();
  const masterKey = compactKey(trailingQty);
  const exactMasterLine = masterKey && masterIndex.get(masterKey);
  return /(?:จำนวน|มี|x|×)?\s*(?:[1-9]|10)(?:\.0)?\s*(?:คอต|คอท|ชิ้น|กล่อง|แพ็ค|แพ็ก|ซอง)/iu.test(s)
    || /(?:คอต|คอท|ชิ้น|กล่อง|แพ็ค|แพ็ก|ซอง)/iu.test(s)
    || Boolean(exactMasterLine);
}

function extractRawLines(row) {
  const p = row.packer_obj || {};
  const itemRows = [ ...(Array.isArray(row.orderItems) ? row.orderItems : []), ...(Array.isArray(p.orderItems) ? p.orderItems : []) ];
  const fromItems = itemRows.map(x => x?.raw_line || x?.raw_text || x?.product_text || x?.sku || '').filter(Boolean).map(String).filter(isProductLineCandidate);
  if (fromItems.length) return [...new Set(fromItems)];
  const sources = [row.sniper_x_text_clean, row.product_text, row.extracted_product_raw, row.product_lines, row.original, row.full_chunk_text, row.chat_text].filter(v => v !== undefined && v !== null && String(v).trim()).map(String).filter(Boolean);
  return [...new Set(sources.flatMap(src => src.split(/\r?\n/).map(x => x.trim()).filter(isProductLineCandidate)))];
}

function rawProductUntilQuantity(raw) {
  const s = String(raw ?? '').normalize('NFKC').trim();
  const beforePrice = s.split(/\|/u)[0].split(/\b\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?\s*(?:THB|บาท)\b/iu)[0].trim();
  const m = beforePrice.match(/^.*?(?:(?:จำนวน|มี)\s*)?\d+(?:\.\d+)?\s*(?:คอต|คอท|ชิ้น|กล่อง|แพ็ค|แพ็ก|ซอง)?/iu);
  return (m?.[0] || beforePrice).replace(/\s+/gu, ' ').trim();
}

function productCandidatesFromRaw(raw) {
  const clean = rawProductUntilQuantity(raw);
  return [...new Set([clean, productPhrase(raw), raw].map(String).map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean))];
}

const mapRawCache = new Map();
function mapOneRaw(raw) {
  const rawSource = String(raw ?? '').trim();
  if (mapRawCache.has(rawSource)) return mapRawCache.get(rawSource);
  const rawDisplay = rawProductUntilQuantity(rawSource);
  const candidates = productCandidatesFromRaw(rawSource);
  let exactMaster = null;
  let matchedCandidate = '';

  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    const key = compactKey(candidate);
    const found = key ? masterIndex.get(key) : undefined;
    if (found) { exactMaster = found; matchedCandidate = candidate; break; }
  }

  let master = exactMaster;
  let method = exactMaster ? 'PRODUCT_MASTER_EXACT_PRODUCT_PHRASE' : '';
  if (!master) {
    for (const candidate of candidates) {
      const found = system2Master(candidate);
      if (found) { master = found; matchedCandidate = candidate; method = 'PRODUCT_MASTER_BRAND_COLOR_OR_FLAVOR'; break; }
    }
  }

  const key = compactKey(matchedCandidate || rawDisplay);

  // 🛡️ หากแมปไม่เจอ คืนค่าดิบกลับไปทันทีโดยไม่มีการตัดทิ้ง (Raw Preservation)
  if (!master) {
    const result = {
      raw_line: rawSource,
      raw_source_line: rawSource,
      normalized_product_key: key,
      sku: null,
      master_sku: null,
      master_display_for_packer: null,
      final_product: rawSource,
      mapped_display: rawSource,
      mapping_status: 'UNMAPPED_KEEP_RAW',
      match_method: key ? 'RAW_PRODUCT_PHRASE_NO_MASTER' : 'NO_PRODUCT_KEY'
    };
    mapRawCache.set(rawSource, result);
    return result;
  }

  // 🎯 หากแมปเจอ: ดึง Master Display พร้อมแกะจำนวนมาต่อคำว่า "คอต" ให้ถูกต้องอัตโนมัติ
  const masterBySku = PRODUCT_MASTER[master.sku] || master;
  const display = masterBySku.master_display_for_packer || `${masterBySku.emoji || ''} ${masterBySku.sku} (${masterBySku.thName || masterBySku.name || ''})`.trim();

// 🧠 ดึงจำนวนจากข้อความดิบให้แม่นยำ รองรับทั้งตัวเลขเดี่ยวๆ ท้ายบรรทัด หรือมีคำว่า คอต/ชิ้น กำกับ
  const qtyMatch = rawSource.match(/(?:จำนวน|มี|x|×)\s*([1-9][0-9]*)(?:\.0)?\s*(?:คอต|คอท|คอต.|กล่อง|แพ็ค|แพ็ก|ซอง)?/iu)
    || rawSource.match(/(?:^|\s)([1-9][0-9]*)(?:\.0)?\s*(?:คอต|คอท|คอต.|กล่อง|แพ็ค|แพ็ก|ซอง)?\s*$/iu)
    || rawSource.match(/(?:^|\s)([1-9][0-9]*)(?:\.0)?\s*$/u);

  const qty = qtyMatch?.[1] || '1'; // ถ้าดึงเจอ เอาค่านั้นมาใช้ ถ้าไม่เจอค่อยตกแต่งเป็น 1
  const canonicalDisplay = `${display} x ${qty} คอต`;


  const result = {
    raw_line: rawSource,
    raw_source_line: rawSource,
    normalized_product_key: key,
    sku: master.sku,
    master_sku: master.sku,
    master_display_for_packer: display,
    final_product: canonicalDisplay,
    mapped_display: canonicalDisplay,
    mapping_status: 'MAPPED',
    match_method: method,
    mapping_system: method.includes('EXACT') ? 'SYSTEM_1_EXACT_PRODUCT_PHRASE' : 'SYSTEM_2_SIGNAL_WORDS',
    unit_price: master.price ?? null
  };

  mapRawCache.set(rawSource, result);
  return result;
}

function cleanAddressCandidate(value) {
  return String(value ?? '')
    .normalize('NFKC')
    .replace(/^\[[^\]]*\]\s*/u, '')
    .replace(/^\d{1,2}[/-]\d{1,2}[/-]\d{2,4}\s+\d{1,2}:\d{2}(?::\d{2})?\]?\s*/u, '')
    .replace(/^(?:ที่อยู่จัดส่ง|ที่อยู่|address)\s*[:：]\s*/iu, '')
    .replace(/^(?:ชื่อผู้รับ|ชื่อ|ผู้รับ)\s*[:：].*?\n/iu, '')
    .replace(/\b(?:โทร|เบอร์โทร|เบอร์มือถือ|มือถือ)\s*[:：]?\s*\+?\d[\d\s().-]{8,}/iu, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/gu, ' ')
    .trim();
}

function addressScore(value) {
  const s = cleanAddressCandidate(value);
  if (!s || /^(?:DATA_MISSING|N\/A|null|undefined)$/iu.test(s)) return -999;
  if (/^[1-9]\d{4}$/u.test(s)) return -999;
  const hasHouse = /(?:บ้านเลขที่|เลขที่|^\d{1,5}(?:\/|-)?\s*\d{0,5}\b|\d+\s*ม\.?)/iu.test(s);
  const hasLocal = /(?:ตำบล|ต\.|แขวง)\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(s);
  const hasDistrict = /(?:อำเภอ|อ\.|เขต)\s*[^\s]+/iu.test(s);
  const hasProvince = /(?:จังหวัด|จ\.|กรุงเทพมหานคร|กรุงเทพฯ|กทม)/iu.test(s);
  const hasZip = /(?<!\d)[1-9]\d{4}(?!\d)/u.test(s);
  const hasLandmark = /(?:วัด|ร้านอาหาร|ร้านกาแฟ|ร้านตัดผม|ร้านค้า|ตลาด|โรงเรียน|บริษัท|หอพัก|รีสอร์ท|โรงแรม|คลินิก|โรงพยาบาล|ปั๊ม|ฟาร์ม|สวน|ซอย|ถนน)/iu.test(s);
  let score = 0;
  if (hasHouse) score += 34;
  if (hasLocal) score += 21;
  if (hasDistrict) score += 21;
  if (hasProvince) score += 21;
  if (hasZip) score += 13;
  if (hasLandmark) score += 8;
  score += Math.min(12, Math.floor(s.length / 20));
  if (hasProvince && !hasHouse && !hasLocal && !hasDistrict && !hasLandmark) score -= 30;
  if (hasZip && !hasHouse && !hasLocal && !hasDistrict && !hasLandmark) score -= 20;
  return score;
}

function getAddress(row) {
  const p = row.source_payload || row.packer_obj || {};
  const rawCandidates = [
    ['rescue_address_normalized', [value(row,'rescue_address_normalized'), value(row,'zipcode')].filter(Boolean).join(' ')],
    ['rescue_address', [value(row,'rescue_address'), value(row,'zipcode')].filter(Boolean).join(' ')],
    ['rescue_address_raw', [value(row,'rescue_address_raw'), value(row,'zipcode')].filter(Boolean).join(' ')],
    ['address_fibo_selected_clean', value(row,'address_fibo_selected_clean')],
    ['full_address', value(row,'full_address')],
    ['final_address_for_bill', value(row,'final_address_for_bill')],
    ['full_address_backup_1', value(row,'full_address_backup_1')],
    ['full_address_backup_2', value(row,'full_address_backup_2')],
    ['finalAddress', value(row,'finalAddress')],
    ['address_display_packer', value(row,'address_display_packer')],
    ['addressclean', value(row,'addressclean')],
    ['address_raw_source_line', value(p,'address_raw_source_line')],
    ['address_line_1', value(row,'address_line_1')],
    ['short_address', value(row,'short_address')],
    ['address', value(row,'address')]
  ];

  for (const source of [row.original, row.full_chunk_text, row.chat_text]) {
    const text = String(source ?? '').replace(/\\n/g, '\n');
    const zip = (text.match(/(?<!\d)[1-9]\d{4}(?!\d)/u) || [])[0] || '';
    for (const line of text.split(/\r?\n/u)) {
      if (/(?:ที่อยู่จัดส่ง|ที่อยู่|address|บ้านเลขที่|เลขที่|หมู่บ้าน|หมู่|ต\.|ตำบล|อ\.|อำเภอ|จ\.|จังหวัด|แขวง|เขต|ถนน|ซอย)\s*[:：=]?/iu.test(line)) {
        const withZip = zip && !/(?<!\d)[1-9]\d{4}(?!\d)/u.test(line) ? `${line} ${zip}` : line;
        rawCandidates.push(['raw_address_text', withZip]);
      }
    }
  }

  const scored = rawCandidates
    .filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined && v !== null && String(v).trim())
    .map(([source, v]) => ({ source, clean: cleanAddressCandidate(v), score: addressScore(v) }))
    .filter(x => x.clean && x.score > -900)
    .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || b.clean.length - a.clean.length);

  return [...new Map(scored.map(x => [x.clean, x])).values()].map(x => x.clean);
}

function formatOrderTime(row) {
  return firstExisting(row, ['order_time_display','orderTimeDisplay','time_th','order_date']) || '';
}

const grouped = new Map();
for (const item of $input.all()) {
  const row = item.json ?? {};
  const key = value(row,'upsert_key') || value(row,'order_number') || value(row,'threadId');
  if (key && !grouped.has(key)) grouped.set(key, row);
}

return [...grouped.values()].map(row => {
  const rawLines = extractRawLines(row);
  const items = rawLines.map(mapOneRaw).filter(x => x.normalized_product_key || x.raw_line);

  const matched = items.filter(x => x.mapping_status === 'MAPPED' || x.mapping_status === 'MATCHED');
  const unmatched = items.filter(x => x.mapping_status !== 'MAPPED' && x.mapping_status !== 'MATCHED');

  const mappedDisplay = matched.map(x => x.mapped_display || x.final_product).filter(Boolean);
  const rawDisplay = unmatched.map(x => x.raw_line).filter(Boolean);

  // รวมรายการที่แมปแล้วกับข้อมูลดิบที่ไม่เจอเข้าด้วยกัน ป้องกันอตกหล่น
  const productDisplay = [...mappedDisplay, ...rawDisplay].join('\n');
  const productFallback = firstExisting(row, [
    'order_fibo_product',
    'packer_tag_display',
    'for_packer_st_display',
    'manus_for_packer',
    'google_for_packer'
  ]);

  const finalProduct = productDisplay || productFallback || '📦 ตรวจสินค้า: ยังไม่พบรายการสินค้า';
  const mappedPrimary = mappedDisplay.join('\n');
  const rawPrimary = rawDisplay.join('\n');
  const sharedProductPrimary = productDisplay || productFallback || '';

  const cod = parseNumber(firstExisting(row,['cod_amount','final_order_price','expected_cod','cod']));
  const addressCandidates = getAddress(row);
  const address = addressCandidates[0] || '';
  const orderTimeDisplay = formatOrderTime(row);
  const orderNumber = value(row,'order_number');
  const header = value(row,'stock_notice');
  const customer = firstExisting(row,['customer_name','facebook_name']);

  const phoneCandidates = Array.isArray(row.phone_candidates) ? row.phone_candidates : [];
  const labeledPhone = phoneCandidates.find(candidate => candidate && typeof candidate === 'object' && candidate.has_phone_label === true && candidate.phone)?.phone || '';
  const phone = firstExisting(labeledPhone, row.extracted_phone, row.phone);

  const message = [
    header,
    '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━',
    orderTimeDisplay ? `⏰ เวลาสั่งซื้อ: ${escapeHtml(orderTimeDisplay)}` : '',
    orderNumber ? `🆔 เลขออเดอร์: ${escapeHtml(orderNumber)}` : '',
    customer ? `👤 ${escapeHtml(customer)}` : '',
    cod != null ? `💰 COD: ${formatBaht(cod)} บาท` : '',
    phone ? `📱 ${escapeHtml(phone)}` : '',
    address ? `📍 ${escapeHtml(address)}` : '',
    '📦 รายการสินค้า:',
    escapeHtml(finalProduct),
    unmatched.length ? `⚠️ รอตรวจสินค้า ${unmatched.length} รายการ` : '',
    '━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━'
  ].filter(Boolean).join('\n');

  return {
    json: {
      ...row,
      customer_message_text: row.customer_message_text ?? row.customer_latest_message ?? null,
      page_message_text: row.page_message_text ?? row.page_latest_message ?? null,
      order_captured: true,
      order_capture_status: 'CAPTURED_KEEP',
      product_quantity_calculation_disabled: true,
      order_time_display: orderTimeDisplay,
      product_items_v5: items,
      product_items_matched: matched,
      product_items_unmatched: unmatched,
      product_map_items: items,
      product_map_result: productDisplay,
      product_map_display: productDisplay,
      product_map_mapped_display: mappedPrimary,
      product_map_raw: rawPrimary,
      product_map_primary: sharedProductPrimary,
      product_map_sku: matched[0]?.sku || '',
      product_map_source_line: items.map(x => x.raw_source_line || x.raw_line).filter(Boolean).join('\n'),
      product_map_count: items.length,
      product_map_mapped_count: matched.length,
      product_map_unmapped_count: unmatched.length,
      product_map_sku_list: matched.map(x => x.sku).filter(Boolean),
      product_map_master_sku_list: matched.map(x => x.master_sku).filter(Boolean),
      product_map_status: matched.length && unmatched.length ? 'PARTIAL_MAPPED_KEEP_RAW' : matched.length ? 'MAPPED' : unmatched.length ? 'UNMAPPED_KEEP_RAW' : 'MISSING_PRODUCT',
      product_mapping_is_blocker: false,
      mapping_status: unmatched.length ? (matched.length ? 'PARTIAL_MAPPED_KEEP_RAW' : 'UNMAPPED_KEEP_RAW') : 'MAPPED',
      mapping_engine_version: 'PRODUCT_MASTER_SYSTEM_1_2_V6_RAW_PRESERVED',
      sku: matched.length === 1 ? matched[0].sku : (matched.length > 1 ? matched.map(x => x.sku).filter(Boolean).join(',') : (row.sku || null)),
      master_sku: matched.length === 1 ? matched[0].master_sku : (matched.length > 1 ? matched.map(x => x.master_sku).filter(Boolean).join(',') : (row.master_sku || null)),

      // ส่งค่าลงฟิลด์สำหรับ Packer ครบถ้วนทุกตัว
     raw_text:$input.first().json.sku,
      display_for_packer: finalProduct,
      final_display_for_packer: finalProduct,
      order_fibo_product: finalProduct,
      packer_tag_display: finalProduct,
      for_packer_st_display: finalProduct,
      manus_for_packer: finalProduct,
      google_for_packer: finalProduct,
      final_product: finalProduct,
      trusted_product: finalProduct,

      product_display_candidates: [finalProduct, ...unmatched.map(x => x.raw_line)].filter(Boolean),
      product_unmatched_raw: unmatched.map(x => x.raw_line),
      address_display_packer: address,
      final_address_for_bill: address,
      address_candidates: addressCandidates,
      cod_amount: cod,
      final_order_price: cod,
      expected_cod: cod,
      telegram_product_display: finalProduct,
      telegram_message: message,
      telegram_copy_text: [orderNumber, orderTimeDisplay, cod != null ? `${formatBaht(cod)} บาท` : '', customer, phone, address, finalProduct].filter(Boolean).join('\n')
    }
  };
});

// ============================================================
// TELEGRAM BILL DECORATOR — หน้าที่แต่งหล่อสร้างข้อความ Telegram เท่านั้น
// ============================================================

function value(r, k) {
  const v = r?.[k];
  return v === undefined || v === null ? "" : String(v).trim();
}

function firstExisting(r, keys) {
  for (const k of keys) {
    const v = value(r, k);
    if (v !== "") return v;
  }
  return "";
}

function parseNumber(raw) {
  if (raw === undefined || raw === null || String(raw).trim() === "") return null;
  const c = String(raw).replaceAll(",", "").replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, "");
  const n = Number(c);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
}

function formatBaht(n) {
  const num = parseNumber(n);
  if (num === null) return "";
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("th-TH", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(num);
}

function escapeHtml(v) {
  return String(v ?? "")
    .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
    .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
    .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
    .replaceAll('"', "&quot;");
}


function htmlToPlainText(value) {
  return String(value ?? "")
    .replace(/<br\s*\/?\s*>/gi, "\n")
    .replace(/<\/(?:p|div|li|tr|h[1-6])\s*>/gi, "\n")
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, "")
    .replace(/&nbsp;/gi, " ")
    .replace(/&#39;|&apos;/gi, "'")
    .replace(/&quot;/gi, '"')
    .replace(/&lt;/gi, "<")
    .replace(/&gt;/gi, ">")
    .replace(/&amp;/gi, "&")
    .replace(/&#(\d+);/g, (_, n) => String.fromCodePoint(Number(n)))
    .replace(/&#x([0-9a-f]+);/gi, (_, n) => String.fromCodePoint(parseInt(n, 16)))
    .replace(/\r\n?/g, "\n")
    .replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, "\n")
    .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")
    .trim();
}

function formatOrderTime(row) {
  const raw = firstExisting(row, ["orderTimeDisplay", "order_time_display", "order_time", "order_date"]);
  if (raw && raw.includes("/")) return raw;
  const iso = firstExisting(row, ["created_at", "updated_at", "orderTimeIso"]);
  if (!iso) return raw;
  try {
    const d = new Date(iso);
    const th = new Date(d.getTime() + 7 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const dd = String(th.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0");
    const mm = String(th.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
    const yyyy = th.getUTCFullYear() + 543;
    const hh = String(th.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, "0");
    const mi = String(th.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, "0");
    return `${dd}/${mm}/${yyyy} ${hh}:${mi} น.`;
  } catch (e) {
    return raw || iso;
  }
}

function resolvePhone(row) {
  if (Array.isArray(row.phone_candidates)) {
    for (const candidate of row.phone_candidates) {
      const phone = String(candidate?.phone ?? '').trim();
      if (phone && phone !== '0') return phone;
    }
  }
  for (const key of ['phone_norm', 'phone_clean', 'extracted_phone', 'phone', 'phone_number', 'customer_phone']) {
    const phone = String(row?.[key] ?? '').trim();
    if (phone && phone !== '0') return phone;
  }
  return '';
}

    header,

      telegram_change_alert_web: telegramChangeAlertWeb,
      bill_header_insert_status: 'HEADER_INSERTED'
    }
  };
});
